Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ecology framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 372. The resulting operator achieves bound O(259/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 372 + O(1/n^259). Summing over all modes and applying the ecology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
